## Research event notes

Parent-only pass across 52 platform/type searches: 303 hits, 299 distinct results. Forty-three selected repositories, 42 additions and one documentation-aggregation deferral. All promotion evidence includes pinned implementation inspection. Inherited Bitsy README and stale TRS-80 checklist reconciled with actual code rather than automatically rejected. Ghidra NROM-only extension and hard-coded parser scope kept explicit. Header-only runtime/wrapper libraries are not standalone compilers or device drivers. X68000 encoding/build constraints retained; guest PCW RAM not confused with host minima. Large PS2 tree read failed and was replaced by targeted extractor traversal. ChatGPT credit in actual Neo Geo palette-generation code supports limited AI disclosure, while MCP capabilities and config alone do not. Candidate code/media remained unexecuted; scope and provenance preserved.
